## ⚠️ Tangerine Whistle

### Run
```bash
uv run execute remote -rA -vv --fork=Prague \
    --rpc-endpoint=http://localhost:37546/ \
    --rpc-seed-key=0xde78ff4e5e77ec2bf28ef7b446d4bec66e06d39b6e6967864b2bf3d6153f3e68 \
    --rpc-chain-id=298 \
    --seed-account-sweep-amount='1_000_000 ether' \
    --default-max-fee-per-blob-gas=710_000_000_000 \
    --tx-wait-timeout=15 \
    --max-tx-per-batch=1 \
    "tests/tangerine_whistle/"
```

### Run Failures

##### ✅ 1. RPC rejects `value` below minimum wei floor (~500 failures)

```
execution_testing.rpc.rpc.SendTransactionExceptionError: JSONRPCError(code=-32602, 
message=[...] Value can't be non-zero and less than 10_000_000_000 wei ...
```

- Affects nearly every `alive_beneficiary` / `has_balance` variant across:
    - `test_selfdestruct_to_account`
    - `test_selfdestruct_state_access_boundary`
    - `test_selfdestruct_to_precompile`
    - `test_selfdestruct_to_precompile_state_access_boundary`
    - `test_selfdestruct_to_system_contract`
    - `test_selfdestruct_to_self`
    - `test_initcode_selfdestruct_to_self`
- **Root cause:** the node/RPC enforces a minimum non-zero `value` of `10_000_000_000` wei (10 gwei — likely a tinybar-rounding constraint on Hedera's EVM). Test fixtures use smaller non-zero balances (e.g. `1` wei), which the client rejects before execution even starts. This is a **client/fixture compatibility issue**, not a SELFDESTRUCT spec bug.

---

##### ✅ 2. Gas price below configured minimum (1 failure)

```
execution_testing.rpc.rpc.SendTransactionExceptionError: JSONRPCError(code=-32009,
message=[...] Gas price '10' is below configured minimum gas price '710000...'
```

- `test_selfdestruct_send_to_sender[fork_Prague-blockchain_test-no_balance]`
- Same family as #1 — node-side minimum enforcement rejecting the fixture before it can run.

---

##### 3. Code not cleared after same-tx SELFDESTRUCT
**Pattern:** `AssertionError: Code of 0x... is 0x<code>ff, expected 0x.`

**Root cause:** Account code isn't wiped after same-tx creation+destruction — bytecode (still ending in the SELFDESTRUCT opcode byte) persists instead of being cleared to `0x`.

**Total:** 90 (76 also fail on Geth, 14 Hedera-only)

##### 4. Nonce mismatch (same-tx + has_balance cases)
**Pattern:** `NonceMismatchError: unexpected nonce for account 0x...: want 0x02, got 0x01`

**Root cause:** Nonce is one less than expected after the same-tx self-destruct + refund/cleanup sequence.

**Total:** 181 (98 also fail on Geth, 83 Hedera-only)

**Also fails on Geth — 98 tests**

##### 5. Balance not zeroed on self-destructing account (pre_deploy + has_balance)
**Pattern:** `BalanceMismatchError: unexpected balance for account 0x...: want 0x00, got 0x02540be400`

**Root cause:** The self-destructing account's balance was expected to be swept to 0 but the full pre-funded balance remains.

**Total:** 58 (54 also fail on Geth, 4 Hedera-only)

##### 6. Precompile / system-contract beneficiary balance mismatches
**Pattern:** `BalanceMismatchError ...(<PRECOMPILE/SYSTEM_CONTRACT_NAME>)...` or `Balance of 0x...0002/0003 is <value>, expected 0.`

**Root cause:** The beneficiary (precompile 0x01-0x11 or system contract) didn't receive funds correctly, or shows leftover/unexpected balance.

**Total:** 38 (38 also fail on Geth, 0 Hedera-only)
